A UX-local Msg\n"},{"number":229,"text":"may govern view-local concerns such as expansion state, sort order,\n"},{"number":230,"text":"filter text, focus movement or draft input. A Msg that changes product\n"},{"number":231,"text":"truth is not merely a UX concern. Process model\n"},{"number":255,"text":"\n"},{"number":256,"text":"The constitutional process model is the Elm Architecture (P0, P1;\n"},{"number":257,"text":"rc.2 #4-process-model-elm-architecture):\n"},{"number":258,"text":"\n"},{"number":259,"text":"- State: the typed state shape, held by the runtime, not by the view;\n"},{"number":260,"text":"- Msg: the typed message algebra naming every action a user or system can\n"},{"number":261,"text":"  emit;\n"},{"number":262,"text":"- Update: the pure reducer of signature `(Msg, State) → (State, Cmd)`; it\n"},{"number":263,"text":"  describes side effects as Cmd values and does not perform them;\n"},{"number":264,"text":"- View: the pure function of signature `State → ViewTree`; given the same\n"},{"number":265,"text":"  state, it produces the same output;\n"},{"number":266,"text":"- Cmd and Sub: typed descriptions of side effects to schedule and of\n"},{"number":267,"text":"  external sources to subscribe to, interpreted by the runtime at the\n"},{"number":268,"text":"  effect membrane.\n"},{"number":269,"text":"\n"},{"number":270,"text":"The runtime owns the loop (P0):\n"},{"number":271,"text":"\n"},{"number":272,"text":"`current State → render View → user emits Msg → Update produces new State and Cmds → runtime interprets Cmds → repeat`\n"},{"number":273,"text":"\n"},{"number":274,"text":"This loop is the Elm Architecture and the constitutional process model of\n"},{"number":275,"text":"UX work under this profile (P1).\n"},{"number":276,"text":"\n"}]},{"end_line":311,"heading":"UX/4. State transitions\n"},{"number":335,"text":"\n"},{"number":336,"text":"State changes through typed messages applied by the pure reducer, the\n"},{"number":337,"text":"Update of §4 (P4a, P1; rc.2 #5-state-transition-law). A UX surface\n"},{"number":338,"text":"declares:\n"},{"number":339,"text":"\n"},{"number":340,"text":"- its State shape;\n"},{"number":341,"text":"- its Msg algebra;\n"},{"number":342,"text":"- its `Update : (Msg, State) → (State, Cmd)`;\n"},{"number":343,"text":"- its Cmd algebra;\n"},{"number":344,"text":"- its Sub algebra when the surface subscribes to external or runtime\n"},{"number":345,"text":"  events.\n"},{"number":346,"text":"\n"},{"number":347,"text":"Each Cmd declaration names (P5, P4b):\n"},{"number":348,"text":"\n"},{"number":349,"text":"- the effect kind;\n"},{"number":350,"text":"- the typed input payload;\n"},{"number":351,"text":"- the admitted carrier or external boundary it targets;\n"},{"number":352,"text":"- the success Msg emitted back to the reducer;\n"},{"number":353,"text":"- the failure Msg emitted back to the reducer;\n"},{"number":354,"text":"- any deterministic correlation key required for replay.\n"},{"number":355,"text":"\n"},{"number":356,"text":"The reducer is pure: it does not perform I/O, log to side channels or\n"},{"number":357,"text":"mutate global state. Effects scheduled by Cmd are interpreted by the\n"},{"number":358,"text":"runtime at the effect membrane (§6) (P0, P1).\n"},{"number":359,"text":"\n"},{"number":360,"text":"View-local ephemera (open or closed flags, hover state, draft input) may\n"},{"number":361,"text":"be held in implementation-local state cells where the framework provides\n"},{"number":362,"text":"them, but only when the value is genuinely view-local: not observed by\n"},{"number":363,"text":"other views, not part of the product's state surface, and not\n"},{"number":364,"text":"consequential after the view unmounts. Product-meaningful state goes\n"},{"number":365,"text":"through the reducer without exception (P6, P3). The discipline test for\n"},{"number":366,"text":"whether state belongs in the reducer or in view-local ephemera: if losing\n"},{"number":367,"text":"the value on unmount changes the product's behaviour or another part of\n"},{"number":368,"text":"the UI's view, it belongs in the reducer (P3).\n"},{"number":369,"text":"\n"}]},{"end_line":391,"heading":"UX/6. Effect membrane","id":"repo://stdo-v3-generation/claude/9aee01ffa76cfbde1a6b8f1153a25bd6d71c216e/source/standards/UX.md#UX%2F6.%20Effect%20membrane","path":"standards/UX.md","sha256":"428a7ca3f8be54b9d7f19dc3ea179d5b817b5e2fc1e834c0a12f13b2b140f69b","start_line":370,"source_file":"/Users/jim/src/apps/specification_methodology/a_c/dogfood/stdo-v3-generation/runs-v2/claude/direct/corpus/standards/UX.md","lines":[{"number":370,"text":"## 6. Effect membrane\n"},{"number":371,"text":"\n"},{"number":372,"text":"Side effects are isolated to a declared effect membrane, which refines\n"},{"number":373,"text":"DESIGN_MODULE §10 Effect edge for UX surfaces (P0, P5;\n"},{"number":374,"text":"rc.2 #6-effect-membrane-rule). The membrane interprets Cmd values\n"},{"number":375,"text":"produced by the reducer and translates side-effect outcomes back into Msg\n"},{"number":376,"text":"values delivered to the reducer. Inside the membrane, I/O, subscriptions\n"},{"number":377,"text":"to external sources, and imperative interop with the host platform (DOM,\n"},{"number":378,"text":"file system, native APIs) are allowed. Outside it, in render code, in\n"},{"number":379,"text":"pure helpers consumed by render, and in the reducer, there is no I/O, no\n"},{"number":380,"text":"subscription and no imperative platform manipulation (P0).\n"},{"number":381,"text":"\n"},{"number":382,"text":"The membrane has one job: interpret declared Cmd values. It is not where\n"},{"number":383,"text":"logic lives. Branching, conditional state derivation and multi-step\n"},{"number":384,"text":"business decisions belong in the reducer; the membrane interprets a\n"},{"number":385,"text":"single declared Cmd per effect (P6). A platform-specific effect handler,\n"},{"number":386,"text":"whatever name the chosen framework gives it (`useEffect`,\n"},{"number":387,"text":"`createEffect`, `onMount`, `subscribe`, a server-action handler), is part\n"},{"number":388,"text":"of the membrane only when it interprets a declared Cmd. A handler that\n"},{"number":389,"text":"contains conditional logic deciding state transitions is a violation: the\n"},{"number":390,"text":"logic moves to the reducer, and the handler interprets one Cmd (P6, P1).\n"},{"number":391,"text":"\n"}]}]}
